# Quantum generative AI reference library

Date: 2026-11-03

Scope: a consolidated reference list for quantum generative AI, covering architectures (quantum GANs, VAEs and autoencoders, diffusion and flow models, Born machines, Boltzmann machines, tensor-network and quantum-inspired models, quantum transformers and sequence models), the quantum-prior and quantum-latent line that R1 and R2 belong to, applications across domains, theory and surveys, tooling and datasets, and quantum work published at AI, computer-vision and graphics venues, from 2016 to November 2026. The list was built by parsing and de-duplicating the paper tables of seven research notes files (quantum_gans_and_vaes, quantum_diffusion_and_flows, born_machines_qbm_transformers, applications, ml_venues, cv_graphics_venues, surveys_theory_priors_tooling); duplicates were merged by arXiv ID, then DOI, then normalized title, keeping the highest priority, the union of tags and the longest summary.

Download rule: every paper with priority must-read or useful from the five topical notes files (quantum_gans_and_vaes, quantum_diffusion_and_flows, born_machines_qbm_transformers, applications, surveys_theory_priors_tooling) was downloaded; from the two venue files (ml_venues, cv_graphics_venues) only must-read papers and useful papers flagged generative yes were downloaded; peripheral papers and the remaining venue papers are listed with links only. No tightening of the rule was needed (the planned count was 386 including R1 and R2). arXiv versions were fetched from export.arxiv.org; non-arXiv open-access PDFs were fetched from the publisher or proceedings page given in the notes. Every file was validated (PDF header and a page count from pdfinfo).

Counts: 664 papers listed, 370 PDFs in the folder (R1 to R370, including the two fixed reference papers), 7 selected papers with no open PDF, 9 download failures, 1 excluded entry plus 12 press releases, total size 1107.4 MB.

Naming convention: R{n}_{arxiv id without version}_{short_snake_case_title}.pdf for arXiv papers and R{n}_{short_snake_case_title}.pdf for papers without an arXiv ID; the short title is at most five lowercase ASCII words. R-numbers follow the order of this list (section order, then year, then first author); R1 and R2 keep their original names. Only downloaded papers carry an R-number.

Legend for the Priority column: must-read (core paper for the topic), useful (worth reading for the topic), peripheral (context only). The Year column shows the year given in the notes; where the notes files disagree (usually the arXiv posting year versus the journal year) both are shown as earliest/latest and the earliest is used for ordering. The Venue column uses the most specific venue string found in the notes. Tags are the union of the notes' tags; in the two venue sections the first tag is the generative flag from the notes.

## 1. Surveys, position papers and theory

Theory of expressivity, trainability, generalisation, quantum advantage and benchmarking, plus surveys, reviews, position papers and theses. Foundational model papers stay in their architecture sections even when the notes also tag them as theory.

## 2. Quantum priors and quantum latent distributions for classical generators

The line R1 and R2 belong to: QCBM, Boltzmann or boson-sampling priors feeding classical GANs, VAEs or decoders, generator-enhanced optimisation, and photonic latent spaces. The section also keeps the classical roots of this line (deep image prior, deep generative prior, associative adversarial networks, latent-complexity theory) and the peripheral photonic-hardware context papers that the notes attached to it.

## 5. Quantum diffusion, score-based, flow matching and normalizing flows

Quantum and hybrid diffusion, score-based, flow-matching and normalizing-flow models. Classical diffusion or flow models applied to quantum-physics tasks are in the Applications section (Quantum-physics use cases).

## 11. Quantum papers at machine-learning venues

Papers collected because they are quantum work accepted at an ML venue (NeurIPS, ICML, ICLR, AAAI, IJCAI, KDD, UAI, AISTATS, COLT, ACL/EMNLP, JMLR, TMLR). Split into generative and other quantum ML, ordered by venue then year. Generative architecture papers at these venues are in their architecture section and listed under See also.
